<script id="moonshift-nasa-sim-models">
/**
 * Deterministic NASA-Grounded Simulation Models
 * Sources:
 * - NASA ECLSS: https://www.nasa.gov/reference/environmental-control-and-life-support-systems-eclss/
 * - NASA ISS Water Recovery (ICES 2024-317): https://ntrs.nasa.gov/api/citations/20240005472/downloads/ICES%202024-317%20Status%20of%20ISS%20Water_Final.pdf
 * - NASA OCHMO ECLSS Brief: https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/eclss-technical-brief-ochmo.pdf
 * - NASA Veggie / APH: https://ntrs.nasa.gov/api/citations/20210022119/downloads/Stars%20APH%20PONDS%20Talk%202021.pdf?attachment=true
 * - NASA Mars Perseverance: https://science.nasa.gov/mission/mars-2020-perseverance/science-instruments/
 * - NASA CCMC DONKI: https://ccmc.gsfc.nasa.gov/tools/DONKI/
 */
window.NasaSimModels = Object.freeze({
    // 1. ECLSS Oxygen Generation Assembly (OGA): 2 H2O -> 2 H2 + O2 (1.125 kg H2O per 1.0 kg O2)
    // Crew demand: ~0.89 kg O2 / crewmember-day; 4-crew habitat = 3.56 kg O2 / day
    simulateECLSS({ crewCount = 4, electrolysisAmps = 45, waterValveOpen = true, cdraBed = 'B' }) {
        const o2RequiredKgDay = +(crewCount * 0.89).toFixed(2);
        const powerKw = waterValveOpen ? +(electrolysisAmps * 0.095).toFixed(2) : 0.2;
        // OGA nominal range: 2.3 to 9.0 kg O2/day
        const o2ProducedKgDay = waterValveOpen ? +((electrolysisAmps / 60) * 5.2).toFixed(2) : 0;
        const h2oConsumedKgDay = +(o2ProducedKgDay * 1.125).toFixed(2);
        const co2Scrubbed = cdraBed === 'B';
        const o2Surplus = +(o2ProducedKgDay - o2RequiredKgDay).toFixed(2);
        const safePressure = electrolysisAmps <= 78;
        const optimal = waterValveOpen && co2Scrubbed && o2ProducedKgDay >= o2RequiredKgDay && safePressure;
        return { o2RequiredKgDay, o2ProducedKgDay, h2oConsumedKgDay, powerKw, co2Scrubbed, o2Surplus, safePressure, optimal };
    },

    // 2. Water Recovery System (WRS: UPA 87% target + WPA 50.8 kg/day nominal, 2.5 L/crew-day hydration)
    simulateWaterRecovery({ brineValveDiverted = false, filterReplaced = false, reactorTempC = 95, tested = false }) {
        const crewHydrationLDay = 4 * 2.5; // 10.0 L/day for 4 crew
        let recoveryPct = 62;
        if (brineValveDiverted) recoveryPct += 12;
        if (filterReplaced) recoveryPct += 13;
        const tempOk = reactorTempC >= 125 && reactorTempC <= 140;
        if (tempOk) recoveryPct += 3;
        let purityPct = 88.4;
        if (brineValveDiverted) purityPct += 4.2;
        if (filterReplaced) purityPct += 4.8;
        if (tempOk) purityPct += 2.4;
        purityPct = +Math.min(99.9, purityPct).toFixed(1);
        const optimal = brineValveDiverted && filterReplaced && tempOk && tested && purityPct >= 99.5;
        return { crewHydrationLDay, recoveryPct, purityPct, tempOk, optimal };
    },

    // 3. Veggie / APH Plant Growth (PPF 0-1000 umol/m2/s, Red 630-660nm + Blue 400-500nm, PONDS water)
    simulateGreenhouse({ ppf = 350, waterLPerHr = 2.4, co2Enriched = true }) {
        // Optimal lettuce/crop range in APH: 250-450 umol/m2/s, 2.0-3.2 L/hr
        const lightKw = +(ppf * 0.022).toFixed(1);
        const lightScore = ppf < 180 ? ppf / 180 : ppf <= 480 ? 1.0 : Math.max(0.4, 1 - (ppf - 480) / 700);
        const waterScore = waterLPerHr < 1.6 ? waterLPerHr / 1.6 : waterLPerHr <= 3.4 ? 1.0 : Math.max(0.5, 1 - (waterLPerHr - 3.4) / 4);
        const co2Mult = co2Enriched ? 1.12 : 0.85;
        const healthPct = Math.round(Math.min(100, (lightScore * 0.52 + waterScore * 0.48) * 92 * co2Mult));
        const biomassRate = +(healthPct * 0.14).toFixed(1);
        const optimal = healthPct >= 82 && lightKw <= 11.5 && waterLPerHr <= 3.4;
        return { lightKw, healthPct, biomassRate, optimal };
    },

    // 4. Mars Rover Terrain & Energy Model (MMRTG 110W + Li-ion battery, slope slip penalty > 18 deg)
    simulateRoverLeg(distM, slopeDeg, terrain) {
        const slipFactor = slopeDeg > 18 ? 1.85 : 1 + (slopeDeg / 30);
        const terrainMult = terrain === 'sand' ? 1.35 : terrain === 'rocky' ? 1.18 : 1.0;
        const energyWh = Math.round(distM * 0.14 * slipFactor * terrainMult);
        const hazard = slopeDeg > 18 || terrain === 'sand';
        return { energyWh, hazard };
    },

    // 5. DONKI Space Weather / SEP Posture (GOES >10 MeV >10 pfu operational threshold)
    simulateSpaceWeather({ locksSecured = 0, mode = 'powered' }) {
        const flareClass = 'X8.7';
        const protonFluxPfu = 42; // Simulated SEP event > 10 pfu operational threshold
        const shieldPct = Math.min(100, locksSecured * 25 + (locksSecured === 3 ? 25 : 0));
        const powerCostKwh = mode === 'powered' ? 8 : 0;
        const timeCostHr = mode === 'manual' ? 1 : 0;
        return { flareClass, protonFluxPfu, shieldPct, powerCostKwh, timeCostHr, ready: locksSecured === 3 };
    }
});
</script>
